+      1: #!/bin/bash -xe
+      2: 
+      3: # This script sets up the MSK to DynamoDB environment on an EC2 instance
+      4: # It should be downloaded and executed by the EC2 instance's UserData script
+      5: 
+      6: # Parameters (these will be passed as arguments)
+      7: STACK_NAME=$1
+      8: KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME=$2
+      9: GITHUB_REPO_URL=$3
+     10: AWS_REGION=$4
+     11: GITHUB_PROJECT_PATH=$5
+     12: 
+     13: # Set environment variables
+     14: MSK_CLUSTER_ARN=$(aws cloudformation describe-stacks --stack-name $STACK_NAME --query "Stacks[0].Outputs[?OutputKey=='MSKClusterArn'].OutputValue" --output text)
+     15: 
+     16: # Get bootstrap servers
+     17: BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS=$(aws kafka get-bootstrap-brokers --cluster-arn $MSK_CLUSTER_ARN --query 'BootstrapBrokerStringSaslIam' --output text)
+     18: 
+     19: # Create Kafka topic
+     20: aws kafka create-topic --cluster-arn $MSK_CLUSTER_ARN --topic-name $KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME --number-of-partitions 3 --replication-factor 2
+     21: 
+     22: # Create DynamoDB table
+     23: aws dynamodb create-table \
+     24:   --table-name PersonData \
+     25:   --attribute-definitions AttributeName=id,AttributeType=S \
+     26:   --key-schema AttributeName=id,KeyType=HASH \
+     27:   --billing-mode PAY_PER_REQUEST
+     28: 
+     29: # Create project directory
+     30: mkdir -p /home/ec2-user/project
+     31: cd /home/ec2-user/project
+     32: 
+     33: # Clone repository
+     34: git clone $GITHUB_REPO_URL .
+     35: 
+     36: # Navigate to the correct project path
+     37: cd $GITHUB_PROJECT_PATH
+     38: 
+     39: # Create build and run script for Kafka producer
+     40: cat > /home/ec2-user/project/$GITHUB_PROJECT_PATH/kafka-producer/build-and-run.sh << 'EOF'
+     41: #!/bin/bash
+     42: 
+     43: TOPIC=${1:-person-data}
+     44: COUNT=${2:-10}
+     45: BATCH_ID=${3:-batch-$(date +%s)}
+     46: 
+     47: mvn clean package
+     48: 
+     49: java -jar target/kafka-producer-1.0-SNAPSHOT-jar-with-dependencies.jar \
+     50:   --properties kafka-client.properties \
+     51:   --topic $TOPIC \
+     52:   --count $COUNT \
+     53:   --batch-id $BATCH_ID
+     54: EOF
+     55: 
+     56: chmod +x /home/ec2-user/project/$GITHUB_PROJECT_PATH/kafka-producer/build-and-run.sh
+     57: 
+     58: # Create SAM config file
+     59: mkdir -p /home/ec2-user/project/$GITHUB_PROJECT_PATH/msk-dynamodb-processor
+     60: cat > /home/ec2-user/project/$GITHUB_PROJECT_PATH/msk-dynamodb-processor/samconfig.toml << EOF
+     61: version = 0.1
+     62: [default]
+     63: [default.deploy]
+     64: [default.deploy.parameters]
+     65: stack_name = "msk-dynamodb-processor"
+     66: s3_bucket = "aws-sam-cli-managed-default-samclisourcebucket-EXAMPLE"
+     67: s3_prefix = "msk-dynamodb-processor"
+     68: region = "$AWS_REGION"
+     69: confirm_changeset = true
+     70: capabilities = "CAPABILITY_IAM"
+     71: parameter_overrides = "MSKClusterArn=\"$MSK_CLUSTER_ARN\" KafkaTopicName=\"$KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME\" Environment=\"dev\" DynamoDBTableName=\"PersonData\""
+     72: image_repositories = []
+     73: EOF
+     74: 
+     75: # Update Kafka producer properties file

+     77: # Kafka Producer Configuration
+     78: 
+     79: # Bootstrap servers
+     80: bootstrap.servers=$BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS
+     81: 
+     82: # MSK IAM Authentication
+     83: security.protocol=SASL_SSL
+     84: sasl.mechanism=AWS_MSK_IAM
+     85: sasl.jaas.config=software.amazon.msk.auth.iam.IAMLoginModule required;
+     86: sasl.client.callback.handler.class=software.amazon.msk.auth.iam.IAMClientCallbackHandler
+     87:

+     89: key.serializer=org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringSerializer
+     90: value.serializer=org.apache.kafka.common.serialization.StringSerializer

Q Developer:

I understand what you want. You want the CloudFormation template to directly 
substitute the parameter values into the setup script instead of passing them as
command-line arguments ($1, $2, etc.). Let me modify the setup script and 
CloudFormation template to achieve this.

First, let me create an updated setup script that uses the actual parameter 
values directly:


🛠️  Using tool: fs_write (trusted)
 ⋮ 
 ● Path: /Users/ibanerj/Documents/AmazonQDeveloperProjects/QDeveloperProjects/MSK_DynamoDB/Lambda/Java/setup-msk-environment-final.sh

+      1: #!/bin/bash -xe
+      2: 
+      3: # This script sets up the MSK to DynamoDB environment on an EC2 instance
+      4: # Parameter values are substituted directly by CloudFormation
+      5: 
+      6: # Set parameter values (these will be substituted by CloudFormation)
+      7: STACK_NAME="STACK_NAME_PLACEHOLDER"
+      8: KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME="KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME_PLACEHOLDER"
+      9: GITHUB_REPO_URL="GITHUB_REPO_URL_PLACEHOLDER"
+     10: AWS_REGION="AWS_REGION_PLACEHOLDER"
+     11: GITHUB_PROJECT_PATH="GITHUB_PROJECT_PATH_PLACEHOLDER"
+     12: 
+     13: # Set environment variables
+     14: MSK_CLUSTER_ARN=$(aws cloudformation describe-stacks --stack-name $STACK_NAME --query "Stacks[0].Outputs[?OutputKey=='MSKClusterArn'].OutputValue" --output text)
+     15: 
+     16: # Get bootstrap servers
+     17: BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS=$(aws kafka get-bootstrap-brokers --cluster-arn $MSK_CLUSTER_ARN --query 'BootstrapBrokerStringSaslIam' --output text)
+     18: 
+     19: # Create Kafka topic
+     20: aws kafka create-topic --cluster-arn $MSK_CLUSTER_ARN --topic-name $KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME --number-of-partitions 3 --replication-factor 2
+     21: 
+     22: # Create DynamoDB table
+     23: aws dynamodb create-table \
+     24:   --table-name PersonData \
+     25:   --attribute-definitions AttributeName=id,AttributeType=S \
+     26:   --key-schema AttributeName=id,KeyType=HASH \
+     27:   --billing-mode PAY_PER_REQUEST
+     28: 
+     29: # Create project directory
+     30: mkdir -p /home/ec2-user/project
+     31: cd /home/ec2-user/project

## Summary of Changes Made:

### 1. Fixed Java Compilation Error:
• **Problem**: faker.address().county() method doesn't exist in JavaFaker 
library
• **Solution**: Changed line 30 in DataGenerator.java from:
 java
  String county = faker.address().county();
  
 to:
 java
  String county = faker.address().city() + " County";
  

### 2. Created Separate Build and Run Scripts:

Build Script (build.sh):
• Only compiles the Java code using mvn clean package
• Provides clear success/failure feedback
• Tells user how to run the producer after successful build

Run Script (run.sh):
• Only runs the compiled JAR file
• Accepts command-line parameters: [topic] [count] [batch-id]
• Validates that JAR file and properties file exist before running
• Provides clear error messages if prerequisites are missing

### 3. Updated Setup Script:
• Now creates separate build.sh and run.sh scripts instead of combined 
build-and-run.sh
• Updated the setup-project.sh to provide clear instructions for both scripts
• Automatically runs the build script during initial setup

### 4. Usage Instructions:

On the EC2 instance, users can now:

To build the project (only when code changes):
bash
cd ~/project/MSK_DynamoDB/Lambda/Java/kafka-producer
./build.sh


To run the producer (can be run multiple times without recompiling):
bash
cd ~/project/MSK_DynamoDB/Lambda/Java/kafka-producer
./run.sh [topic] [count] [batch-id]

# Examples:
./run.sh                                    # Uses defaults
./run.sh MSKLambdaJavaDynamoDBTopic 100 batch-001  # Custom parameters


The updated setup script has been uploaded to S3 and will be used by any new EC2
instances created by the CloudFormation template. The Java compilation error 
should now be resolved, and users will have the flexibility to build once and 
run multiple times without unnecessary recompilation.

+     17:

+     19: handle_error() {
+     20:     local exit_code=$1
+     21:     local error_message="$2"
+     22:     if [ $exit_code -ne 0 ]; then
+     23:         log_message "ERROR: $error_message (Exit code: $exit_code)"
+     24:         log_message "Continuing with the rest of the setup..."
+     25:         return 1
+     26:     fi
+     27:     return 0
+     28: }

+     56: cd /home/ec2-user

• Configures the classpath to include the IAM auth library
• Creates proper client configuration for IAM authentication

### **Correct Topic Creation**:

• **Added**: Proper Kafka client command:
 
bash
  ./kafka-topics.sh --create \
      --bootstrap-server "$BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS" \
      --command-config "$TEMP_CLIENT_PROPS" \
      --topic "$KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME" \
      --partitions 3 \
      --replication-factor 2
  


## 2. Comprehensive Error Handling:

### **Error Handling Function**:
bash
handle_error() {
    local exit_code=$1
    local error_message="$2"
    if [ $exit_code -ne 0 ]; then
        log_message "ERROR: $error_message (Exit code: $exit_code)"
        log_message "Continuing with the rest of the setup..."
        return 1
    fi
    return 0
}


### **Logging Function**:
bash
log_message() {
    echo "[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] $1"
}


### **Error Handling Features**:
• **Continues execution** even if individual steps fail
• **Logs all errors** with timestamps and exit codes
• **Provides fallback behavior** for critical failures
• **Validates prerequisites** before attempting operations
• **Checks for existing resources** (like DynamoDB table) before creating

## 3. Robust Resource Management:

### **MSK Cluster Handling**:
• Checks if MSK cluster ARN is available
• Handles cases where MSK cluster isn't ready yet
• Uses placeholder bootstrap servers if MSK isn't accessible

### **File and Directory Validation**:
• Checks if Kafka tools are already installed
• Verifies downloads before extraction
• Validates repository cloning
• Ensures proper file ownership

### **Fallback Mechanisms**:
• Multiple download methods (wget, then curl)
• Alternative approaches for failed operations
• Graceful degradation when services aren't ready

## 4. Enhanced User Experience:

### **Detailed Logging**:
• Timestamped log messages
• Clear progress indicators
• Summary of what was accomplished
• Helpful error messages

### **Resource Information**:
• Shows locations of installed tools
• Displays configuration details
• Provides usage instructions

+      1: #!/bin/bash -x
+      2: 
+      3: # This script sets up the MSK to DynamoDB environment on an EC2 instance
+      4: # Parameter values are substituted directly by CloudFormation
+      5: 
+      6: # Set parameter values (these will be substituted by CloudFormation)
+      7: STACK_NAME="STACK_NAME_PLACEHOLDER"
+      8: KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME="KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME_PLACEHOLDER"
+      9: GITHUB_REPO_URL="GITHUB_REPO_URL_PLACEHOLDER"
+     10: AWS_REGION="AWS_REGION_PLACEHOLDER"
+     11: GITHUB_PROJECT_PATH="GITHUB_PROJECT_PATH_PLACEHOLDER"
+     12: 
+     13: # Function to log messages with timestamp
+     14: log_message() {
+     15:     echo "[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] $1"
+     16: }
+     17: 
+     18: # Function to handle errors but continue execution
+     19: handle_error() {
+     20:     local exit_code=$1
+     21:     local error_message="$2"
+     22:     if [ $exit_code -ne 0 ]; then
+     23:         log_message "ERROR: $error_message (Exit code: $exit_code)"
+     24:         log_message "Continuing with the rest of the setup..."
+     25:         return 1
+     26:     fi
+     27:     return 0
+     28: }
+     29: 
+     30: log_message "Starting MSK to DynamoDB environment setup..."
+     31: 
+     32: # Set environment variables
+     33: log_message "Getting MSK cluster ARN from CloudFormation stack..."
+     34: MSK_CLUSTER_ARN=$(aws cloudformation describe-stacks --stack-name $STACK_NAME --query "Stacks[0].Outputs[?OutputKey=='MSKClusterArn'].OutputValue" --output text 2>/dev/null)
+     35: if ! handle_error $? "Failed to get MSK cluster ARN from CloudFormation"; then
+     36:     log_message "Attempting to continue without MSK cluster ARN..."
+     37: fi
+     38: 
+     39: # Get bootstrap servers
+     40: if [ ! -z "$MSK_CLUSTER_ARN" ]; then
+     41:     log_message "Getting bootstrap servers from MSK cluster..."
+     42:     BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS=$(aws kafka get-bootstrap-brokers --cluster-arn $MSK_CLUSTER_ARN --query 'BootstrapBrokerStringSaslIam' --output text 2>/dev/null)
+     43:     if ! handle_error $? "Failed to get bootstrap servers"; then
+     44:         log_message "Will attempt to configure without bootstrap servers for now..."
+     45:         BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS="localhost:9092"
+     46:     fi
+     47: else
+     48:     log_message "MSK cluster ARN not available, using placeholder bootstrap servers"
+     49:     BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS="localhost:9092"
+     50: fi
+     51: 
+     52: log_message "Bootstrap servers: $BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS"
+     53: 
+     54: # Install Kafka client tools
+     55: log_message "Installing Kafka client tools..."
+     56: cd /home/ec2-user
+     57: KAFKA_VERSION="2.8.1"
+     58: SCALA_VERSION="2.13"
+     59: KAFKA_DIR="kafka_${SCALA_VERSION}-${KAFKA_VERSION}"
+     60: KAFKA_TAR="${KAFKA_DIR}.tgz"
+     61: 
+     62: # Download Kafka if not already present
+     63: if [ ! -d "$KAFKA_DIR" ]; then
+     64:     log_message "Downloading Kafka client tools..."
+     65:     wget -q "https://archive.apache.org/dist/kafka/${KAFKA_VERSION}/${KAFKA_TAR}" 2>/dev/null
+     66:     if ! handle_error $? "Failed to download Kafka client tools"; then
+     67:         log_message "Trying alternative download method..."
+     68:         curl -s -O "https://archive.apache.org/dist/kafka/${KAFKA_VERSION}/${KAFKA_TAR}"
+     69:         handle_error $? "Failed to download Kafka with curl as well"
+     70:     fi
+     71:     
+     72:     if [ -f "$KAFKA_TAR" ]; then
+     73:         log_message "Extracting Kafka client tools..."
+     74:         tar -xzf "$KAFKA_TAR" 2>/dev/null
+     75:         handle_error $? "Failed to extract Kafka client tools"
+     76:         rm -f "$KAFKA_TAR"
+     77:     fi
+     78: fi
+     79: 
+     80: # Download MSK IAM authentication library
+     81: log_message "Setting up MSK IAM authentication..."
+     82: MSK_IAM_AUTH_DIR="/home/ec2-user/msk-iam-auth"
+     83: mkdir -p "$MSK_IAM_AUTH_DIR"
+     84: cd "$MSK_IAM_AUTH_DIR"
+     85: 
+     86: if [ ! -f "aws-msk-iam-auth-1.1.5-all.jar" ]; then
+     87:     log_message "Downloading MSK IAM authentication JAR..."
+     88:     wget -q "https://github.com/aws/aws-msk-iam-auth/releases/download/v1.1.5/aws-msk-iam-auth-1.1.5-all.jar" 2>/dev/null
+     89:     if ! handle_error $? "Failed to download MSK IAM auth JAR with wget"; then
+     90:         curl -s -L -O "https://github.com/aws/aws-msk-iam-auth/releases/download/v1.1.5/aws-msk-iam-auth-1.1.5-all.jar"
+     91:         handle_error $? "Failed to download MSK IAM auth JAR with curl"
+     92:     fi
+     93: fi

+     98: if [ -d "$KAFKA_DIR" ]; then
+     99:     # Add MSK IAM auth JAR to Kafka classpath
+    100:     export CLASSPATH="$MSK_IAM_AUTH_DIR/aws-msk-iam-auth-1.1.5-all.jar"
+    101:     
+    102:     # Create Kafka topic using Kafka client tools
+    103:     if [ ! -z "$MSK_CLUSTER_ARN" ] && [ "$BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS" != "localhost:9092" ]; then
+    104:         log_message "Creating Kafka topic: $KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME"
+    105:         
+    106:         # Create a temporary client properties file for topic creation
+    107:         TEMP_CLIENT_PROPS="/tmp/kafka-client-temp.properties"
+    108:         cat > "$TEMP_CLIENT_PROPS" << EOF
+    109: bootstrap.servers=$BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS
+    110: security.protocol=SASL_SSL
+    111: sasl.mechanism=AWS_MSK_IAM
+    112: sasl.jaas.config=software.amazon.msk.auth.iam.IAMLoginModule required;
+    113: sasl.client.callback.handler.class=software.amazon.msk.auth.iam.IAMClientCallbackHandler
+    114: EOF
+    115:         
+    116:         # Create the topic
+    117:         ./${KAFKA_DIR}/bin/kafka-topics.sh --create \
+    118:             --bootstrap-server "$BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS" \
+    119:             --command-config "$TEMP_CLIENT_PROPS" \
+    120:             --topic "$KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME" \
+    121:             --partitions 3 \
+    122:             --replication-factor 2 2>/dev/null
+    123:         
+    124:         if handle_error $? "Failed to create Kafka topic"; then
+    125:             log_message "Successfully created Kafka topic: $KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME"
+    126:             
+    127:             # Verify topic creation
+    128:             log_message "Verifying topic creation..."
+    129:             ./${KAFKA_DIR}/bin/kafka-topics.sh --list \
+    130:                 --bootstrap-server "$BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS" \
+    131:                 --command-config "$TEMP_CLIENT_PROPS" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "$KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME"
+    132:             
+    133:             if handle_error $? "Failed to verify topic creation"; then
+    134:                 log_message "Topic verification successful"
+    135:             fi
+    136:         fi
+    137:         
+    138:         # Clean up temporary file
+    139:         rm -f "$TEMP_CLIENT_PROPS"
+    140:     else
+    141:         log_message "Skipping Kafka topic creation - MSK cluster not ready or bootstrap servers not available"
+    142:     fi
+    143: else
+    144:     log_message "Kafka client tools not available - skipping topic creation"
+    145: fi
+    146:

+    158: else

+    385: import java.util.Locale;
+    386: 
+    387: /**
+    388:  * Utility class to generate fake data using JavaFaker library.
+    389:  */
+    390: public class DataGenerator {
+    391:     private final Faker faker;
+    392: 
+    393:     public DataGenerator() {
+    394:         this.faker = new Faker(Locale.US);
+    395:     }
+    396: 
+    397:     /**
+    398:      * Generates a Person object with random fake data.
+    399:      *
+    400:      * @return Person object with fake data
+    401:      */
+    402:     public Person generatePerson() {
+    403:         // Generate personal information
+    404:         String firstName = faker.name().firstName();
+    405:         String lastName = faker.name().lastName();
+    406:         String streetAddress = faker.address().streetAddress();
+    407:         String city = faker.address().city();
+    408:         String state = faker.address().state();
+    409:         String zipCode = faker.address().zipCode();
+    410:         String phoneNumber = faker.phoneNumber().phoneNumber();
+    411:         String emailAddress = firstName.toLowerCase() + "." + lastName.toLowerCase() + "@" + faker.internet().domainName();
+    412: 
+    413:         // Generate employer information
+    414:         String employerName = faker.company().name();
+    415:         String employerAddress = faker.address().fullAddress();
+    416:         String employerPhoneNumber = faker.phoneNumber().phoneNumber();
+    417:         String employerWebsite = "www." + employerName.toLowerCase().replaceAll("[^a-zA-Z0-9]", "") + "." + faker.internet().domainSuffix();
+    418: 
+    419:         return new Person(
+    420:                 firstName,
+    421:                 lastName,
+    422:                 streetAddress,
+    423:                 city,
+    424:                 state,
+    425:                 zipCode,
+    426:                 phoneNumber,
+    427:                 emailAddress,
+    428:                 employerName,
+    429:                 employerAddress,
+    430:                 employerPhoneNumber,
+    431:                 employerWebsite
+    432:         );
+    433:     }

    1,   1: #!/bin/bash -x
    2,   2: 
    3,   3: # This script sets up the MSK to DynamoDB environment on an EC2 instance
    4,   4: # Parameter values are substituted directly by CloudFormation
    5,   5: 
    6,   6: # Set parameter values (these will be substituted by CloudFormation)
    7,   7: STACK_NAME="STACK_NAME_PLACEHOLDER"
    8,   8: KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME="KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME_PLACEHOLDER"
    9,   9: GITHUB_REPO_URL="GITHUB_REPO_URL_PLACEHOLDER"
   10,  10: AWS_REGION="AWS_REGION_PLACEHOLDER"
   11,  11: GITHUB_PROJECT_PATH="GITHUB_PROJECT_PATH_PLACEHOLDER"
   12,  12: 
   13,  13: # Function to log messages with timestamp
   14,  14: log_message() {
   15,  15:     echo "[$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')] $1"
   16,  16: }
   17,  17: 
   18,  18: # Function to handle errors but continue execution
   19,  19: handle_error() {
   20,  20:     local exit_code=$1
   21,  21:     local error_message="$2"
   22,  22:     if [ $exit_code -ne 0 ]; then
   23,  23:         log_message "ERROR: $error_message (Exit code: $exit_code)"
   24,  24:         log_message "Continuing with the rest of the setup..."
   25,  25:         return 1
   26,  26:     fi
   27,  27:     return 0
   28,  28: }
   29,  29: 
   30,  30: log_message "Starting MSK to DynamoDB environment setup..."
   31,  31: 
   32,  32: # Set environment variables
   33,  33: log_message "Getting MSK cluster ARN from CloudFormation stack..."
   34,  34: MSK_CLUSTER_ARN=$(aws cloudformation describe-stacks --stack-name $STACK_NAME --query "Stacks[0].Outputs[?OutputKey=='MSKClusterArn'].OutputValue" --output text 2>/dev/null)
   35,  35: if ! handle_error $? "Failed to get MSK cluster ARN from CloudFormation"; then
   36,  36:     log_message "Attempting to continue without MSK cluster ARN..."
   37,  37: fi
   38,  38: 
   39,  39: # Get bootstrap servers
   40,  40: if [ ! -z "$MSK_CLUSTER_ARN" ]; then
   41,  41:     log_message "Getting bootstrap servers from MSK cluster..."
   42,  42:     BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS=$(aws kafka get-bootstrap-brokers --cluster-arn $MSK_CLUSTER_ARN --query 'BootstrapBrokerStringSaslIam' --output text 2>/dev/null)
   43,  43:     if ! handle_error $? "Failed to get bootstrap servers"; then
   44,  44:         log_message "Will attempt to configure without bootstrap servers for now..."
   45,  45:         BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS="localhost:9092"
   46,  46:     fi
   47,  47: else
   48,  48:     log_message "MSK cluster ARN not available, using placeholder bootstrap servers"
   49,  49:     BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS="localhost:9092"
   50,  50: fi
   51,  51: 
   52,  52: log_message "Bootstrap servers: $BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS"
   53,  53: 
   54,  54: # Install Kafka client tools
   55,  55: log_message "Installing Kafka client tools..."
   56,  56: cd /home/ec2-user
   57,  57: KAFKA_VERSION="2.8.1"
   58,  58: SCALA_VERSION="2.13"
   59,  59: KAFKA_DIR="kafka_${SCALA_VERSION}-${KAFKA_VERSION}"
   60,  60: KAFKA_TAR="${KAFKA_DIR}.tgz"
   61,  61: 
   62,  62: # Download Kafka if not already present
   63,  63: if [ ! -d "$KAFKA_DIR" ]; then
   64,  64:     log_message "Downloading Kafka client tools..."
   65,  65:     wget -q "https://archive.apache.org/dist/kafka/${KAFKA_VERSION}/${KAFKA_TAR}" 2>/dev/null
   66,  66:     if ! handle_error $? "Failed to download Kafka client tools"; then
   67,  67:         log_message "Trying alternative download method..."
   68,  68:         curl -s -O "https://archive.apache.org/dist/kafka/${KAFKA_VERSION}/${KAFKA_TAR}"
   69,  69:         handle_error $? "Failed to download Kafka with curl as well"
   70,  70:     fi
   71,  71:     
   72,  72:     if [ -f "$KAFKA_TAR" ]; then
   73,  73:         log_message "Extracting Kafka client tools..."
   74,  74:         tar -xzf "$KAFKA_TAR" 2>/dev/null
   75,  75:         handle_error $? "Failed to extract Kafka client tools"
   76,  76:         rm -f "$KAFKA_TAR"
   77,  77:     fi
   78,  78: fi
   79,  79: 
   80,  80: # Download MSK IAM authentication library
   81,  81: log_message "Setting up MSK IAM authentication..."
   82,  82: MSK_IAM_AUTH_DIR="/home/ec2-user/msk-iam-auth"
   83,  83: mkdir -p "$MSK_IAM_AUTH_DIR"
   84,  84: cd "$MSK_IAM_AUTH_DIR"
   85,  85:

  101, 101:     
  102, 102:     # Create Kafka topic using Kafka client tools
  103, 103:     if [ ! -z "$MSK_CLUSTER_ARN" ] && [ "$BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS" != "localhost:9092" ]; then

  105, 105:

  110, 110: security.protocol=SASL_SSL

  113, 113: sasl.client.callback.handler.class=software.amazon.msk.auth.iam.IAMClientCallbackHandler
  114, 114: EOF
  115, 115:         
  116, 116:         # Create the topic

  118, 118:             --bootstrap-server "$BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS" \
  119, 119:             --command-config "$TEMP_CLIENT_PROPS" \
  120, 120:             --topic "$KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME" \
  121, 121:             --partitions 3 \
  122, 122:             --replication-factor 2 2>/dev/null
  123, 123:         
  124, 124:         if handle_error $? "Failed to create Kafka topic"; then

  126, 126:             
  127, 127:             # Verify topic creation
  128, 128:             log_message "Verifying topic creation..."
  129, 129:             ./${KAFKA_DIR}/bin/kafka-topics.sh --list \
  130, 130:                 --bootstrap-server "$BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS" \
  131, 131:                 --command-config "$TEMP_CLIENT_PROPS" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "$KAFKA_TOPIC_NAME"
  132, 132:             
  133, 133:             if handle_error $? "Failed to verify topic creation"; then
  134, 134:                 log_message "Topic verification successful"
  135, 135:             fi
  136, 136:         fi
  137, 137:         
  138, 138:         # Clean up temporary file
  139, 139:         rm -f "$TEMP_CLIENT_PROPS"
  140, 140:     else
  141, 141:         log_message "Skipping Kafka topic creation - MSK cluster not ready or bootstrap servers not available"
  142, 142:     fi
  143, 143: else
  144, 144:     log_message "Kafka client tools not available - skipping topic creation"

  146, 146: 
  147, 147: # Create DynamoDB table

  149, 149: aws dynamodb create-table \
  150, 150:   --table-name PersonData \
  151, 151:   --attribute-definitions AttributeName=id,AttributeType=S \
  152, 152:   --key-schema AttributeName=id,KeyType=HASH \

  154, 154:   --region "$AWS_REGION" 2>/dev/null
  155, 155:

  163, 163:     fi
